Bu çekirdek, yalnızca belirli kullanımlarda bıçağın sapını, mali yükümlülük doğuran alt eşiği, kişinin köken ve soyunu ya da güneşin batış yerini gösterir.","distinctive_facets":[{"facet_id":"F001","role":"core","statement":"Bir şeyin dayandığı temel, köken, dönüş noktası veya sabit başvuru ölçüsü."},{"facet_id":"F002","role":"specialization","statement":"Bıçağın elde tutulan sapı veya arka bölümü."},{"facet_id":"F003","role":"specialization","statement":"Malın belirli bir mali yükümlülüğü doğurduğu sabit alt miktar."},{"facet_id":"F004","role":"specialization","statement":"Bir kişinin geldiği köken, yetiştiği soy ve bu kökene dayanan saygınlık."},{"facet_id":"F005","role":"specialization","statement":"Güneşin gün sonunda döner gibi görünüp battığı yer."}],"identity_rationale":"Dal, temel, dönüş noktası ve sabit ölçü düşüncesini taşıyan kullanımları doğru toplar; ancak bunlar tek bir yalın ve her bağlama uygulanabilir anlam değildir. Bıçak sapı, mali yükümlülük eşiği, soy kökeni ve güneşin batış yeri yalnızca kendi söz kalıpları içinde korunmalıdır.","lexical_glosses":[{"lexical_unit_id":"lu_021","rendering_kind":"ordinary","target_gloss":"bir şeyin temeli ve dönülen başvuru noktası"},{"lexical_unit_id":"lu_022","rendering_kind":"ordinary","target_gloss":"bıçağın sapı veya arka bölümü"},{"lexical_unit_id":"lu_023","rendering_kind":"ordinary","target_gloss":"mal için mali yükümlülük doğuran alt miktar"},{"lexical_unit_id":"lu_024","rendering_kind":"ordinary","target_gloss":"köken, soy ve aileden gelen saygınlık"},{"lexical_unit_id":"lu_025","rendering_kind":"ordinary","target_gloss":"güneşin battığı ve döndüğü yer"}],"lexicalization_note":"Tanım ortak temel ve sabit başvuru noktasını verir; bıçak, mal, soy ve güneşle kurulan anlamları ayrı ve kalıba bağlı uzmanlaşmalar olarak tutar.","neighbor_coverage_note":"Bütün adaylar değerlendirildi; hesap, ağırlık ve ölçme dalları yalnızca sabit miktar yüzünü, köken dalları ise yalnızca temel yüzünü paylaşır. Bu nedenle yayımlanan ilişki eş anlamlılık değil aynı alan karşılaştırmasıdır.","neighbor_distinctions":[{"boundary_match":"field_only","distinction":"Ortak köken alanına rağmen odak dal farklı nesnelerde sabit başvuru ve eşik anlamları taşıyan bir kullanım ailesidir; komşu dalın nesne kapsamı ayrıdır.","focus_only":"Odak dal, kökenin yanı sıra bıçak sapı, mali eşik ve güneşin batış yeri gibi kalıplaşmış başvuru noktalarını kapsar.","gloss":"köken ve çıkış yeri","neighbor_only":"Komşu dal, kişinin soy kökenini ve hörgücün kök bölümünü kendi sözlüksel alanında adlandırır.","neighbor_ref":"root_000340/B005","relation_type":"same_field","shared_zone":"Her iki dal da bir varlığın geldiği temel veya köken noktasını gösterebilir."}],"source_phrase_ar":"نصاب الشيء أصله؛ نصاب السكين؛ بلغ المال النصاب الذي تجب فيه الزكاة (maqayis)؛ نصاب كل شيء أصله ومرجعه؛ رجع إلى مركبه ومنصبه أي أصل منبته وحسبه؛ نصاب الشمس مغيبها (ayn)؛ نصاب السكين؛ نصاب صدق أي حسب ثابت (jamhara)؛ المنصب الأصل وكذلك النصاب؛ النصاب من المال القدر الذي تجب فيه الزكاة؛ نصاب السكين مقبضه (sihah)؛ نصاب كل شيء أصله ومرجعه؛ نصاب الشمس مغيبها؛ أنصبت السكين جعلت لها نصابا (tahdhib)؛ نصاب السكين ونصبه؛ نصاب الشيء أصله؛ رجع فلان إلى منصبه أي أصله (mufradat)","source_summary":"Ortak malzeme temel ve geri dönülen başvuru noktası çevresinde toplanır, fakat kullanımlar güçlü biçimde sözlüksel sınırlıdır. HFT visibility is governed by the authoring policy above.","docket_mode":"quarantine_without_hft","docket_ready":true}},"selected_context_units":[],"support_registry":[{"branch_refs":["root_001507/B009"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-6","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_07840a9707cc62c1d39b","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:7:3\",\"root_001507\",[\"B009\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Yüksek sesli ezgi çağrışımı, daha önce yükseltilen ve dilde dolaşan anılmayı yeni çabanın sesli bir parçası hâline getirebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B006","root_001507/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-2","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_2078dbb3efbbcdabdd13","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:7:2\",\"root_001147\",[\"B006\"]],[\"94:7:3\",\"root_001507\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Boşalma, belirli bir işe bütünüyle yönelme; doğrulma ise son ayetin gösterdiği yöne sağlam ve görünür biçimde yerleşme anlamı kazanır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B002","root_001507/B001"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-3","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_4da6722361280633874e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:7:2\",\"root_001147\",[\"B002\"]],[\"94:7:3\",\"root_001507\",[\"B001\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Bir kabın dökülerek boşaltılması ve sonra dikilmesi çağrışımları, kişiyi rakip içeriklerden arındırılıp bütünüyle yöneltilen bir kap gibi gösterebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B002"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:3","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_5951f515ad77a60a2f55","text":"3. **Exploratory: emptying a vessel and installing it upright.** Fixed `ف ر غ/B002` is pouring out and emptying a vessel, while fixed `ن ص ب/B001` is erecting or fixing an object. The earlier burden has been removed, and the following prepositional direction toward the Lord gives the erected form an orientation. The branches supply emptying and installation. **Abductive move:** I infer the self as the unstated vessel/object whose competing contents are poured out before it is set upright; the material identification is not in the packet. **Reading change:** the command becomes a compact empty-and-orient ritual of the whole person.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_73f1cde3b115a58e6e6c","text":"1. **Completion reallocates capacity to fresh toil.** Fixed `ف ر غ/B001` is freedom or emptiness after occupation, and fixed `ن ص ب/B004` is fatigue, strain, and hard work. The prior two ease statements create available capacity, but the paired conditional and command immediately assign it again. The first branch supplies released attention or energy; the second supplies its next use. **Abductive move:** I infer completion -> freed capacity -> deliberate reinvestment in labor; the packet supplies succession but not an energy model. **Reading change:** finishing is a transfer point between exertions, not an invitation to remain vacant.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B006"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_7c803ad8930da9e06630","text":"94:8 supplied the destination missing from every branch of 94:7. It therefore sharpened `ف ر غ/B006` as deliberate devotion, turned `ن ص ب/B001` from mere uprightness into oriented establishment, and made the `ف ر غ/B003` plus `ن ص ب/B010` travel reading a directed journey rather than undirected motion.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_000582/B010","root_001147/B001","root_001147/B002","root_001147/B006","root_001507/B001","root_001507/B003","root_001507/B009","root_001657/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_7f7030b62c1188e70042","text":"1. Completion leads to renewed upright exertion. Lexical evidence: `ف ر غ B001` emptiness/freedom after occupation or completion of work; `ن ص ب B001` setting something upright/prominent and `ن ص ب B004` weariness, strain, toil. Structural cue: after relief and repeated ease (94:5-6), the imperative comes with `فـ` and `إذا`. Assigned role: `فرغت` marks emptied/completed capacity; `فانصب` turns that capacity into upright work. Abductive move supplied by me: ease is not an endpoint but the condition that makes new exertion possible. Reading change: the ayah reads as a rhythm of completion and re-engagement rather than rest after hardship. 2. The vessel-pouring branch makes the chest-opening concrete. Lexical evidence: `ف ر غ B002` pouring out and emptying a vessel; earlier `ش ر ح B001` opening and `ص د ر B001` chest; `و ض ع B001/B002` setting down and delivery. Structural cue: the passage opened the chest, removed a load, then says “when you have emptied/finished.” Assigned role: the person is like a vessel emptied of constricting contents, ready to be set upright. Abductive move supplied by me: inner relief is modeled as evacuation before reorientation. Reading change: the ayah becomes a post-emptying instruction, not merely scheduling. 3. A deliberate-turning reading bridges to final desire. Lexical evidence: `ف ر غ B006` turning deliberately to a matter/devoting oneself; `ر غ ب B001` desire turning toward or away; `ر ب ب B001/B002` lordship and nurture. Structural cue: 94:7 immediately precedes `وَإِلَىٰ رَبِّكَ فَٱرْغَب`. Assigned role: `فراغ` is not vacancy but undivided availability for the next object. Abductive move supplied by me: the command to set oneself up is incomplete until the next ayah supplies the direction. Reading change: the ayah reads as focused readiness for devotion. 4. The upright-marker/cistern reading gives the imperative spatial form. Lexical evidence: `ن ص ب B003` upright marker/boundary or cistern stones; `س م و B004` overhead sky/cover including rain, `ر ب ب B008/B013` low cloud and abundant gathered water, `ف ر غ B002` pouring. Structural cue: water/cloud/pouring branches cluster around the final half of the passage once `فرغ` appears. Assigned role: `انصب` sets a marker or rim after pouring, making a bounded place for reception. Abductive move supplied by me: the opened/emptied self becomes a prepared site rather than a spent vessel. Reading change: the imperative can be spatially read as establishing a receiving place. 5. Raised-voice chant is activated by the prior raised mention. Lexical evidence: `ن ص ب B009` raised-voice song/chant; `ر ف ع B010` highness of voice; `ذ ك ر B004` mention on the tongue. Structural cue: `رفعنا لك ذكرك` precedes `فانصب`. Assigned role: the renewed action may include voiced praise/proclamation. Abductive move supplied by me: when mention has been raised, the next upright exertion can be vocal as well as bodily. Reading change: the ayah includes performative utterance, not only labor.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B001","root_001507/B004"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-1","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_980f6480776e9ed8819e","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:7:2\",\"root_001147\",[\"B001\"]],[\"94:7:3\",\"root_001507\",[\"B004\"]]],\"grade\":\"strong\",\"text\":\"Önceki kolaylıkla açılan dikkat ve güç, boş bırakılmak yerine yeni bir zahmete aktarılır; bitiriş, iki çalışma dönemi arasında kapasitenin yeniden tahsis edildiği bir geçiştir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":[],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:retrospective_surprises_md:1","source_type":"v12_reader_walks","support_id":"sup_98f4473539e96eb2d319","text":"94:8 completed 94:7. On first pass `فانصب` could point toward exertion, uprightness, marker, chant, or fatigue; `وَإِلَىٰ رَبِّكَ فَٱرْغَب` supplied the missing directional arrow. The action is not mere busyness after free time but a setting-up of the whole emptied self toward the Lord. The earlier `رفعنا لك ذكرك` also kept surprising this ayah: `نصب` as raised-voice song became more plausible because mention had already been raised.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B002","root_001507/B003"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-5","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a4eac88483f850e1c236","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:7:2\",\"root_001147\",[\"B002\"]],[\"94:7:3\",\"root_001507\",[\"B003\"]]],\"grade\":\"reject\",\"text\":\"Boşaltılan kap ile dik sınır veya havuz taşı çağrışımları, buyruğu yeni olanı almaya hazırlanmış sınırlı bir kabul yeri kurmak gibi gösterebilir.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B003","root_001507/B010"],"citable":true,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"finding-4","source_type":"cross_run_publication","support_id":"sup_a682a7a3d3464364f41d","text":"{\"anchors\":[[\"94:7:2\",\"root_001147\",[\"B003\"]],[\"94:7:3\",\"root_001507\",[\"B010\"]]],\"grade\":\"weak\",\"text\":\"Genişleyen yürüyüş ve gün boyu süren yolculuk çağrışımları, yükü alınmış yolcunun son ayette belirtilen hedefe doğru yeniden yola çıkmasını canlandırır.\"}","trust":"trusted"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B006","root_001507/B001"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:2","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fa991c50c2c8414a9db0","text":"2. **Deliberate devotion after attention is cleared.** Fixed `ف ر غ/B006` is intentionally turning or devoting oneself to a particular matter; fixed `ن ص ب/B001` is setting something upright and prominent. The next ayah directs desire **إلى ربك**, furnishing an explicit orientation. The فرغ branch supplies concentrated intent, and the نصب branch supplies embodied establishment. **Abductive move:** I infer that the imperative establishes the addressee in the direction named in 94:8. **Reading change:** **فرغت** can activate intentional single-purpose devotion, and **فانصب** becomes taking up a firm, visible stance for it.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"branch_refs":["root_001147/B003"],"citable":false,"role":"candidate_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_a:activated_readings_md:4","source_type":"v12_reader_walks_wide","support_id":"sup_fe2a39c4760f75131082","text":"4. **A journey resumed with a sustained march.** Fixed `ف ر غ/B003` is breadth in a horse's gait or road, and fixed `ن ص ب/B010` is a full-day, relatively easy march. Behind them, 94:3 activates the worn mount (`ن ق ض/B002`, `ظ ه ر/B005`) and 94:5-6 activate pliant motion (`ي س ر/B005`); 94:8 supplies the journey's heading. The fixed branches provide opened stride and sustained travel. **Abductive move:** I infer that unloading and ease restore the same traveler's gait, allowing movement toward the final destination. **Reading change:** the imperative can be heard as “take the opened road and set out,” alongside its exertional reading.","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only. HFT-stated segmentation, word indices, roots, branches, and roles remain attributed nominations unless independently supplied elsewhere in this packet.","cited_anchor_count":0,"exact_arabic_is_surface_evidence_only":true,"missing_anchor_refs":[],"supplied_anchor_count":0,"target_morphology_supplied":false},"branch_refs":[],"payload":"The removed crushing load and the new imperative separate imposed exhaustion from chosen exertion while preserving an upright recovery reading.","qualification":{"anchor_scope_complete":true,"authoring_effect":"Source qualifications affect epistemic status and containment, never visibility or presumptive outcome.","packet_scope_relation":"exact_declared_pericope","reader_identity_status":"legacy_unbound","record_warnings":["Unanchored reader synthesis must be assessed as one inventory item, not accepted as a surah thesis."]},"role":"hft_nomination_evidence","scope":"global","source_local_id":"reader_hft_a:reader_summary:strongest_changes/0","source_type":"hft","support_id":"sup_eeb039329698c48f0d47","trust":"legacy_unbound"},{"anchor_evidence":[],"anchor_evidence_coverage":{"boundary":"Exact Arabic verifies surface contact only.
